([zademy/lu-query](https://github.com/zademy/lu-query/tree/HEAD/skills/lu-query-explore-schema) · ★2)
- **lu-query-manage-schema**: Use lu-query to change Oracle structure in the user's own schema - comments on tables and columns, views, synonyms, sequences, indexes and materialized-view logs. - **lu-query-plan-data-changes**: Use lu-query to change Oracle rows safely - preview the blast radius with execute_dml_preview, rehearse with execute_dml_rollback_first, commit with execute_dml, and pick new keys with find_free_id or get_next_sequence_value. Use for INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/MERGE where the user approved the change. ([zademy/lu-query](https://github.com/zademy/lu-query/tree/HEAD/skills/lu-query-plan-data-changes) · ★2)
- **lu-query-query-data**: Use lu-query to answer a data question from Oracle with a read-only SELECT - run_query for arbitrary SELECTs with offset/limit pagination, get_sample_data for a quick row preview. ([zademy/lu-query](https://github.com/zademy/lu-query/tree/HEAD/skills/lu-query-query-data) · ★2)
- **lu-query-triage-incident**: Use lu-query to triage an Oracle database incident - oracle_mcp_health_report for a readiness overview, list_active_sessions/list_blocked_sessions/list_locks/get_wait_events/get_session_longops for live sessions, get_session_sql_text and list_top_sql for what is running and what is expensive, get_instance_info/get_database_info/get_parameter/get_system_stats/get_db_time_model/list_datafile_io for the system, plus get_session_privs and get_session_roles. - **lu-query-tune-sql**: Use lu-query to diagnose slow or suspicious Oracle SQL - explain_plan for the real execution plan without running the statement, suggest_index for index DDL when a full scan is the problem, get_table_stats/get_column_stats/get_histogram for optimizer statistics, list_unused_indexes for index cleanup and run_sql_tuning_advisor for the advisor report. - **lu-query-use-plsql**: Use lu-query to inspect and call Oracle PL/SQL - describe_plsql for arguments and source, call_procedure for procedures/functions/package subprograms with IN/OUT/INOUT parameters and SYS_REFCURSOR results, get_plsql_errors for compile failures.
